| 352 | `02_System_reference/02.11_Data_Conversion_and_Bridges/02.11.02.02.01.02.01_Structure_of_the_GROUPS_Statements.md` | `37473.htm` | #0 | 5×3 | 13 | 2 | `\| Timing Set Changes \| Timing Set Changes \| Effects \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\| SUBPAT Group \| MAIN pattern \| Effects \|⏎\| NCT \| NCT \| - CTIM is inserted into the MAIN pattern before the first line of the ASCII vector table instead of the default position before the first vector (by adding INITIAL_CTIM in the pattern configuration file (*.vbc)). This takes care of an ASCII vector table starting with a subpattern call(JSUB).<br>- The default insertion of CTIM before the first vector is suppressed for all subpatterns of the group (by specifying the v2b option -T for them in the vector configuration file (*.aiv)). \|⏎\| CT \| CT \| - CTIM is inserted before the first vector into all patterns of the group except those subpatterns that also occur in a SUBPAT group with NCT.<br>- CTIM is inserted into the MAIN pattern after every JSUB instruction (by adding the v2b option -t to theMAIN pattern in the vector configuration file (*.aiv)).<br>- If required, CTIM is inserted into the MAIN pattern before calling those subpatterns of the group that also occur in a SUBPAT group withNCT (by adding CTIM_BEFORE_JSUB for these subpatterns in the pattern configuration file (*.vbc) of the MAIN pattern). \|⏎\| CT \| NCT \| - CTIM is inserted into the MAIN pattern before the first line of the ASCII vector table instead of the default position before the first vector (by adding INITIAL_CTIM in the pattern configuration file (*.vbc)). This takes care of an ASCII vector table starting with a subpattern call(JSUB).<br>- CTIM is inserted before the first vector in all subpatterns of the group except those that also occur in a SUBPAT group with NCT.<br>- If required, CTIM is inserted into the MAIN pattern after calling a subpattern of the group (by adding CTIM_AFTER_JSUB for these subpatterns in the pattern configuration file (*.vbc) of the MAIN pattern).<br>- If required, CTIM is inserted into the MAIN pattern before calling a subpattern of the group that also occurs in a SUBPAT group with NCT (by adding CTIM_BEFORE_JSUB for this subpattern in the pattern configuration file (*.vbc) of the MAIN pattern). \|⏎⏎> ⚠️ 转录说明（非原文）：本表在上游 HTML 里含合并格（colspan / rowspan）；⏎> 跨格文字已按覆盖范围重复写出（**不是原表里有重复的格子**）。真结构见 `tdc-html/raw/37473.htm`。` |

| 356 | `03_Use_cases_and_application_papers/03.02_How_to_do_Multi-Site_Testing/03.02.03.04.05_PACT__PACT__-__Parallel_ACTivities_control.md` | `98678_3.htm` | #0 | 7×3 | 16 | 5 | `\| control \| The control parameter is: \| The control parameter is: \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\|  \| ACTIVE \| When a site is ACTIVE it receives all test data, and generates results for the tests. \|⏎\|  \| MASKED \| When a site is MASKED, it receives all test data, as with the ACTIVE parameter. However, the receivers for the site are disabled and no result information is available. \|⏎\| site \| A single integer representing a site number. \| A single integer representing a site number. \|⏎\| site_string \| A string consisting of "0" and "1". One character must be given for every site that is configured in the pin configuration. "0" indicates that the respective site is (to be) masked. "1" indicates that the respective site is (to be) activated. \| A string consisting of "0" and "1". One character must be given for every site that is configured in the pin configuration. "0" indicates that the respective site is (to be) masked. "1" indicates that the respective site is (to be) activated. \|⏎\| ALL \| means that all configured sites that are available for testing (that is, do not use a channel that is inactivated in the MCDIgnore file) are to be activated or masked.<br><br>For the MCDIgnore file see MCDIgnore usage and restrictions in Service Guide Part 1. \| means that all configured sites that are available for testing (that is, do not use a channel that is inactivated in the MCDIgnore file) are to be activated or masked.<br><br>For the MCDIgnore file see MCDIgnore usage and restrictions in Service Guide Part 1. \|⏎\| STR \| forces the query to return a site string instead of a single line for every site. \| forces the query to return a site string instead of a single line for every site. \|⏎⏎> ⚠️ 转录说明（非原文）：本表在上游 HTML 里含合并格（colspan / rowspan）；⏎> 跨格文字已按覆盖范围重复写出（**不是原表里有重复的格子**）。真结构见 `tdc-html/raw/98678_3.htm`。` |
| 357 | `03_Use_cases_and_application_papers/03.03_How_to_use_Multiport_for_specific_Applications/03.03.04.04.02_FTCG__FTCG__-__Functional_Test_ConfiGure.md` | `98606_5.htm` | #0 | 6×3 | 13 | 5 | `\| parameter \| The parameter to be used is SKPR (SKip PRimaries). \| The parameter to be used is SKPR (SKip PRimaries). \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\| mode \| Specifies different activation conditions. This option can take the following values: \| Specifies different activation conditions. This option can take the following values: \|⏎\| mode \| ON \| Suppresses activation of all primary sets at the start of a functional test for the sequencing groups of the ports specified by the portlist parameter. This option serves to keep a PLL alive across functional tests. \|⏎\| mode \| OFF \| Allows activation for the ports specified by the portlist parameter. This is the default. \|⏎\|  \| NEVER \| By this option, the primary sets will always be loaded at the start of a test for all ports of the sequencing groups to which the ports specified by the portlist parameter belong. \|⏎\| portlist \| In the command, specify a list of ports, separated by commas. Note that the ports are treated as representatives of their sequencing groups. Activation will be suppressed, allowed, or enforced not only for the specified ports, but for all member ports of the sequencing groups to which the specified ports belong in the current setup.<br><br>In the query, specify @@, which stands for all ports. \| In the command, specify a list of ports, separated by commas. Note that the ports are treated as representatives of their sequencing groups. Activation will be suppressed, allowed, or enforced not only for the specified ports, but for all member ports of the sequencing groups to which the specified ports belong in the current setup.<br><br>In the query, specify @@, which stands for all ports. \|⏎⏎> ⚠️ 转录说明（非原文）：本表在上游 HTML 里含合并格（colspan / rowspan）；⏎> 跨格文字已按覆盖范围重复写出（**不是原表里有重复的格子**）。真结构见 `tdc-html/raw/98606_5.htm`。` |
